# Fix model.editor and model.document references in all test files

## User

Fix all compilation errors in the test files under ~/code/rust-editor/tests/ caused by changing `model.document` and `model.editor` from fields to accessor methods.

Changes needed throughout test files:
1. `model.editor.*` (read) → `model.editor().*`
2. `model.editor.* = value` (write) → `model.editor_mut().* = value`
3. `model.document.*` (read) → `model.document().*`
4. `model.document.* = value` (write) → `model.document_mut().* = value`

Common patterns:
- `model.editor.cursor()` → `model.editor().cursor()`
- `model.editor.cursor_mut()` → `model.editor_mut().cursor_mut()`
- `model.editor.viewport.visible_lines` → `model.editor().viewport.visible_lines`
- `model.editor.viewport.top_line = x` → `model.editor_mut().viewport.top_line = x`
- `model.editor.selections` → `model.editor().selections`
- `model.document.buffer` → `model.document().buffer`
- `model.document.line_count()` → `model.document().line_count()`
- `model.document.cursor_to_offset()` → `model.document().cursor_to_offset()`

Test files to check:
- tests/cursor_movement.rs
- tests/text_editing.rs
- tests/selection.rs
- tests/scrolling.rs
- tests/edge_cases.rs
- tests/monkey_tests.rs
- tests/status_bar.rs

After making all fixes, run: cargo test --no-run 2>&1 | grep "error\[" | head -30
to verify no errors remain.

Return a summary of files changed and the number of fixes made.
